me_thaqafi_007 = {
	name = "al-Hajjaj" # al-Ḥajjaj ibn Yusuf ath-Thaqafi, Infamous Umayyad governor of Iraq
	dynasty = me_banu_thaqif
	religion = "ashari"
	culture = "bedouin"
	trait = education_martial_4
	trait = callous
	trait = wrathful # 'The shedding of blood came as easily to him as breathing air'
	trait = vengeful # Spitful and envious, as he described himself
	trait = deceitful # Often used cunning and deception to win wars
	trait = zealous # Curiously enough, The very same sources that cursed him and called him a disbeliever made great reference to the strength of his faith
	trait = loyal # He was extremely loyal to al-Walid, who viewed him very favorably
	trait = lifestyle_poet

	father = me_thaqafi_008
	mother = me_thaqafi_014
	661.1.1 = {
		birth = yes
	}
	680.1.1 = {
		effect = { # He was a Hafidh, and was known for his reverance of the Qur'an
			add_trait = hafidh
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = hafidh
				value = 100
			}
		}
	}
	714.1.1 = {
		death = { # Died due to an illness, the cause of which is disputed 
			death_reason = death_ill
		}
	}
}

me_thaqafi_015 = {
	name = "Muhammad" # Brother of al-Hajjaj and governor of San'a
	dynasty = me_banu_thaqif
